# MHA SP2 — Ultra Age evidence reconciliation records, part 1

This bounded storage partition belongs to the [canonical supplemental reconciliation ledger](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#ultra-age-checkpoint). Each stable record has one detailed owner; a partition is not another witness or project entrypoint. Earlier-book links resolve through the canonical index.

## Ultra Age checkpoint

Records inherit edition, authorship and date limits from the [UAG audit](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_ULTRA_AGE_PARATEXT_AUDIT.md#edition-authorship-and-inherited-time-metadata). The [spine map](../01%20Source%20Lock%20and%20Inventory/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_SPINE_MAP.tsv) and [coverage](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_UAG_PAGE_COVERAGE.csv) resolve exact source member, image hash and visibly verified printed folio.

Source admission is distinct from current-home propagation, which remains controlled by the ordered reconciliation and impact audit. Destination links assign responsibility without claiming an update or a readiness promotion. NEW means newly admitted within this source boundary, not first-ever publication or an independent behavioral trial. The audience is the guidebook reader unless a depicted participant/addressee is specified. Original dates of individual contributions remain unknown unless expressly stated.

**Attribution / domain:** Unsigned Ultra Age editorial text; individual attribution may be specified in the claim Subjects: Source publication apparatus. Domains: Edition and temporal authority.

**Adjudication:** EDITORIAL_SUMMARY; analytical **NARROW**; temporal **UNRESOLVED**; modeling **NONE**. Publication notice, not a narrative event or an individual contribution date.

**Dependence and comparison:** Publisher apparatus; no independent behavioral observation. Current-home comparison and limitations are stated in the consequence; exact manga-panel mapping is not newly claimed. Earlier-book comparison: [SUP-UA-002-01](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-ua-002-01); [SUP-UAN-002-01](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-002-01); [SUP-UAN-293-01](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-293-01). UA002, UAN002 and UAN293 already acknowledge digital re-editing. UAG repeats the package-provenance caution; repetition does not identify the date of every contribution or create another source witness.

**Consequence and limit:** Do not make the package a real-time2026 character update or infer that all reused art and statements were first made at the same date. The original EPUB and reading derivative remain distinct containers of the same source images. Confidence: High for the cited readable content; stated source and chronology limits retained.

### SUP-UAG-010-01

**Source and claim:** `UAG:s0010`. The luminous-baby account begins from its public news, while Quirkless people are explicitly said sometimes to suffer discrimination/persecution, a social problem. Japanese anchor: 「ニュースを発端に／差別や迫害を受ける事もあり、社会問題となっている」.

**Attribution / domain:** Unsigned Ultra Age editorial text; individual attribution may be specified in the claim Subjects: Luminous baby; Midoriya Izuku; Quirkless people. Domains: Public origin story and exclusion.

**Adjudication:** EDITORIAL_SUMMARY/NARRATIVE; analytical **NARROW**; temporal **UNRESOLVED**; modeling **FACTUAL_TEXTURE**. Mixed dawn-of-Quirks account, childhood diagnostic panels and general social present; not a dated endpoint survey.

**Dependence and comparison:** Dependent re-expression of manga material with editorial framing. The news trigger does not establish the chronologically first Quirk-bearing person. Current-home comparison and limitations are stated in the consequence; exact manga-panel mapping is not newly claimed. Earlier-book comparison: [SUP-UAN-285-02](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-285-02). UAN285 disappearance rhetoric concerns prejudice against Quirks in its CRC entry. UAG010 explicitly adds Quirkless discrimination, a related but different target; this alone is not a same-population contradiction. UAG011 and UAN053 supply the direct heteromorph comparison.

**Consequence and limit:** Retain the difference between a publicly recognized beginning and later hidden-origin knowledge. The world guide names exclusion as a social problem; it cannot support a blanket claim that modern prejudice has vanished. It supplies no prevalence estimate for discrimination. Confidence: High for the cited readable content; stated source and chronology limits retained.

**Responsible current homes / disposition:** [S3](../05%20Specialist%20Synthesis/MHA_SP2_POWER_INHERITANCE_AND_SELF_AUTHORSHIP_SYNTHESIS.md), [S4](../05%20Specialist%20Synthesis/MHA_SP2_HERO_SOCIETY_AND_DISTRIBUTED_RESPONSIBILITY_SYNTHESIS.md), [L1](../05%20Specialist%20Synthesis/MHA_SP2_MIDORIYA_IZUKU_CHARACTER_MONOGRAPH.md), [F2](../06%20Full-Series%20Synthesis/MHA_SP2_RESCUE_POWER_PERSONHOOD_ADJUDICATION.md), [F4](../06%20Full-Series%20Synthesis/MHA_SP2_FORM_REVISION_AND_RESIDUALS.md).

### SUP-UAG-011-01

**Source and claim:** `UAG:s0011`. The guide distinguishes emitter, transformation and heteromorph categories while allowing mixed cases; Fat Gum is explicitly emitter/transformation and Tokoyami's Dark Shadow is classified as a special heteromorph case. It states that distinctive appearances can encounter social discrimination/prejudice. Japanese anchor: 「複合型／差別や偏見に直面する事も」.

UAN053 explicitly preserves regional heteromorph prejudice while UAN285 broadly says Quirk prejudice disappeared. UAG011 preserves appearance-based discrimination and thus the narrower scope; it supplies no dated adult-endpoint prevalence.

**Consequence and limit:** Do not infer moral character from bodily category or assume only one type per person. This general acknowledgment of ongoing prejudice narrows the earlier UAN glossary's disappearance rhetoric, but does not by itself settle how much discrimination survives in the adult endpoint. ### SUP-UAG-014-01

**Source and claim:** `UAG:s0014`. The school/licensing route includes written and practical evaluation, then provisional powers for emergencies. The depicted exam assesses combat, information processing and lifesaving; its first phase reduces1540 candidates to100 and the rescue phase requires at least50points. Japanese anchor: 「緊急時に限り／戦闘力・情報処理能力・救命力」.

**Attribution / domain:** Unsigned Ultra Age editorial text; individual attribution may be specified in the claim Subjects: Midoriya Izuku; Hero students; Mera Yokumiru. Domains: Evaluation, permission and institutional selection.

**Adjudication:** EDITORIAL_SUMMARY/NARRATIVE; analytical **CONFIRM**; temporal **EARLIER_ONLY**; modeling **NONE**. The exam taken by Midoriya's cohort is explicitly the numerical example. General June/September, three-venue description is not newly demonstrated for every year.

**Dependence and comparison:** Retold original exam; no fresh test data or simulation validation. Current-home comparison and limitations are stated in the consequence; exact manga-panel mapping is not newly claimed. Earlier-book comparison: [SUP-UAN-283-02](MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-283-02). UAN28302 distinguishes internships and limited emergency provisional authority. UAG supplies specific examination figures for the depicted selection, not the universal form of every year test.
